238: function validateServiceAccount(value) {
239:   return Boolean(
240:     value && value.type === "service_account" &&
241:     typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
242:     typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
243:     value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
244:     value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
245:   );
246: }

358: async function schoolReadPost(action,payload={}){
359:   const url=await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();
360:   const response=await net.fetch(url,{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json;charset=UTF-8','accept':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({action,...payload})});
361:   let data={};try{data=await response.json();}catch{}
362:   if(!response.ok)throw schoolReadError(data?.message||('School Read API HTTP '+response.status),data?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_HTTP_ERROR');
363:   return data;
364: }
365: async function loginSchoolReadUser({name='',email=''}={}){
366:   const deviceId=await getOrCreateSchoolReadDeviceId();
367:   const result=await schoolReadPost('login',{name:String(name||'').trim(),email:String(email||'').trim(),deviceId});
368:   if(result?.ok&&result?.token){await saveSchoolReadSession({token:String(result.token),deviceId,user:result.user||{},expiresAt:Date.now()+Math.max(300,Number(result.expiresIn||0))*1000,savedAt:new Date().toISOString()});liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;}
369:   return result;
370: }
371: async function schoolReadSessionStatus({verify=true}={}){
372:   let endpointReady=true,endpointError='';
373:   try{await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();}catch(error){endpointReady=false;endpointError=String(error?.message||error);}
374:   const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
375:   if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)return {ok:endpointReady,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:endpointError,user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};
376:   if(!verify)return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady,user:session.user||null,mode:'school_read_api'};
377:   try{const result=await schoolReadPost('verify-token',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId});if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'UEP 세션 확인 실패',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_TOKEN_INVALID');session.user=result.user||session.user||{};await saveSchoolReadSession(session);return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady:true,user:session.user,expiresAt:result.expiresAt||'',mode:'school_read_api'};}catch(error){await clearSchoolReadSession();return {ok:false,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:String(error?.message||error),code:error?.code||'',user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};}
378: }
379: async function schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges){
380:   const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
381:   if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)throw schoolReadError('UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_LOGIN_REQUIRED');
382:   const result=await schoolReadPost('batch-read',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId,spreadsheetId,ranges});
383:   if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BATCH_FAILED');
384:   return result.valueRanges||[];
385: }

511:   let settled=false;

518:       const returnedState=String(u.searchParams.get('state')||'');
519:       const code=String(u.searchParams.get('code')||'');
520:       const error=String(u.searchParams.get('error')||'');
521:       res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store'});
522:       if(error||returnedState!==state||!code){
523:         res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>UEP Google 계정 연결이 취소되었거나 실패했습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP에서 다시 시도해 주세요.</p>');

632: async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
633:   if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
634:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
635:   url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
636:   url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
637:   url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
638:   ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
639:   const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
640:   if (!response.ok) {
641:     let detail = "";
642:     try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
643:     if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
644:       const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
645:       const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
646:       await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
647:       return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
648:     }
649:     throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
650:   }
651:   return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
652: }

190: function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
191:   if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
192:   if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
193:   if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
194:   if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
195:   if(typeof value==='object'){
196:     const out={};
197:     for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
198:       if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
199:       out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
200:     }
201:     return out;
202:   }
203:   return String(value);
204: }

208: }

13005: // __UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BADGE_08122__
13006: (function installSchoolReadAuthBadgeSync(){
13007:   async function syncSchoolReadAuthBadge(){
13008:     try{
13009:       if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus)return;
13010:       const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:false});
13011:       const authenticated=Boolean(status?.authenticated);
13012:       const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,span,div,a')];
13013:       for(const node of nodes){
13014:         const text=String(node.textContent||'').trim();
13015:         if(authenticated && text==='인증 필요'){
13016:           node.dataset.uepSchoolReadAuthBadge='1';
13017:           node.textContent='연결됨';
13018:           node.title='UEP School Read API 인증 정상';
13019:         }else if(!authenticated && node.dataset?.uepSchoolReadAuthBadge==='1' && text==='연결됨'){
13020:           node.textContent='인증 필요';
13021:           node.title='UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.';
13022:         }
13023:       }
13024:     }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP] School Read auth badge sync failed',error);}
13025:   }
13026:   if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{syncSchoolReadAuthBadge();setInterval(syncSchoolReadAuthBadge,5000);},{once:true});
13027:   else {syncSchoolReadAuthBadge();setInterval(syncSchoolReadAuthBadge,5000);}
13028: })();

13005: // __UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BADGE_08122__
13006: (function installSchoolReadAuthBadgeSync(){
13007:   async function syncSchoolReadAuthBadge(){
13008:     try{
13009:       if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus)return;
13010:       const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:false});
13011:       const authenticated=Boolean(status?.authenticated);
13012:       const nodes=[...document.querySelectorAll('button,span,div,a')];
13013:       for(const node of nodes){
13014:         const text=String(node.textContent||'').trim();
13015:         if(authenticated && text==='인증 필요'){
13016:           node.dataset.uepSchoolReadAuthBadge='1';
13017:           node.textContent='연결됨';
13018:           node.title='UEP School Read API 인증 정상';
13019:         }else if(!authenticated && node.dataset?.uepSchoolReadAuthBadge==='1' && text==='연결됨'){
13020:           node.textContent='인증 필요';
13021:           node.title='UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.';
13022:         }
13023:       }
13024:     }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP] School Read auth badge sync failed',error);}
13025:   }
13026:   if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{syncSchoolReadAuthBadge();setInterval(syncSchoolReadAuthBadge,5000);},{once:true});
13027:   else {syncSchoolReadAuthBadge();setInterval(syncSchoolReadAuthBadge,5000);}
13028: })();
